View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop a novel method for multiplex nucleic acid testing that eliminates the need for target-specific reporters or probes, addressing the challenge of distinguishing multiple co-amplified targets within a single reaction.
Design and population
The study involved a proof-of-concept implementation using recombinase polymerase amplification (RPA) to generate amplicons from Monkeypox (Mpox), Cowpox, Vaccinia, and human RNase P. A held-out validation dataset of 23 samples and a separate blinded cohort of 62 samples were used for model evaluation.
Core methods
The method utilized primer-programmed amplicon design to generate target-associated amplicons with predefined lengths, which were then analyzed using glass nanopores. Individual translocation events were classified by a deep neural network (DNN) based on a six-dimensional feature set derived from nanopore recordings. Primer quality was assessed using established guidelines and open-source software.
Main results
Event-level decoding achieved >95% accuracy, >94.7% sensitivity, and >98.3% specificity. Sample-level calls showed >93.3% sensitivity and >93.9% specificity in blinded samples, with qualitative calls matching polymerase chain reaction (PCR) results across 9 PCR-benchmarked samples.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret these results as demonstrating a probe-free multiplexing architecture that uses primer-defined amplicon design and deep learning decoding, offering new opportunities for all-electrical nucleic acid testing without the need for target-specific reporter chemistry.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide a novel approach for multiplex nucleic acid testing that could improve the efficiency and accuracy of diagnostic methods for Mpox and related viruses, supporting better surveillance and rapid identification in clinical and public health settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's results are based on a limited number of samples and nanopores, and the model's performance was evaluated on a blinded cohort not used for training. The evidence is moderate, as the methodology is promising but requires further validation in larger and more diverse datasets.

Journal: Reviews in medical virology  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-01  |  DOI: 10.1002/rmv.70215  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Future scheduled publication  |  Citation: 2026, 36, 6, e70215
A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

特考韦瑞 对人类 猴痘病毒 感染的 治疗 的功效:随机对照试验的系统回顾和荟萃分析。

Efficacy of Tecovirimat for the Treatment of Mpox Virus Infection in Humans: Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis of Randomized Controlled Trials.
作者： Davi M Grecco, Luciano Araldi, Giordano C Soldera, João A B Zimmermann, João V Brisolara, Giovanna A Artero, Luiza F Streher, Maria E F Turatti, Harapan Harapan
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述评估了 特考韦瑞 对人类 猴痘病毒 感染的 治疗 的有效性和安全性,重点关注临床结果,例如皮肤病变临床消退的时间、第 28-29 天的病变消退以及 28 天全因 死亡率。
证据基础与方法
对随机对照试验 (RCT) 进行了系统回顾和荟萃分析,检索了从开始到 2026 年 6 月 5 日的 PubMed、Embase 和 Cochrane 对照试验中央注册库。使用 RoB 2 评估偏倚风险,并使用 GRADE 评估证据的确定性。使用具有限制最大似然估计和 Hartung-Knapp-Sidik-Jonkman 调整的随机效应模型汇总危险比 (HR) 和风险比 (RR)。
共识与结论
Tecovirimat did not significantly shorten the time to clinical resolution of skin lesions compared with placebo (HR 1.10, 95% CI 0.52-2.31, GRADE: moderate) or increase lesion resolution by day 28-29 (RR 1.00, 95% CI 0.92-1.08, GRADE: high). Virological clearance, pain-related outcomes, and serious adverse events were also comparable between treatment groups.
争议与缺口
未观察到统计异质性(两种结果的 I 2 = 0%)。然而,第 28 天的 死亡率 在这两项试验中都很罕见,排除了荟萃分析。该综述强调需要进一步的证据来确定选定的高危人群是否可以从 抗病毒药物 治疗中受益。
科研与实践启示
目前的随机证据不支持常规使用 特考韦瑞 来加速 猴痘 患者的临床恢复或提高 猴痘 率。需要进一步研究来探索其对特定高危人群的潜在益处。

Efficacy of Tecovirimat for the Treatment of Mpox Virus Infection in Humans: Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis of Randomized Controlled Trials.

Authors: Davi M Grecco, Luciano Araldi, Giordano C Soldera, João A B Zimmermann, João V Brisolara, Giovanna A Artero, Luiza F Streher, Maria E F Turatti, Harapan Harapan
Original abstract (English)
Tecovirimat has been widely used for the treatment of mpox despite limited evidence supporting its efficacy. We conducted a systematic review and meta-analysis of randomized controlled trials (RCTs) to evaluate the efficacy and safety of tecovirimat for this purpose. PubMed, Embase, and the Cochrane Central Register of Controlled Trials were searched from inception to June 5, 2026 for RCTs comparing tecovirimat with placebo or standard care in patients with laboratory-confirmed mpox. Primary outcomes were time to clinical resolution of skin lesions, clinical resolution by day 28-29, and 28-day all-cause mortality. Hazard ratios (HRs) and risk ratios (RRs) were pooled using random-effects models with restricted maximum likelihood estimation and Hartung-Knapp-Sidik-Jonkman adjustment. Risk of bias was assessed using RoB 2 and certainty of evidence using GRADE. Two low-risk-of-bias RCTs involving 941 participants were included. Tecovirimat did not shorten the time to clinical resolution of skin lesions compared with placebo (HR 1.10, 95% CI 0.52-2.31, GRADE: moderate) or increase the lesion resolution by day 28-29 (RR 1.00, 95% CI 0.92-1.08, GRADE: high). No statistical heterogeneity was observed (I 2 = 0% for both outcomes). Mortality at day 28 was rare in both trials, precluding meta-analysis. Virological clearance, pain-related outcomes, and serious adverse events were also comparable between treatment groups. In conclusion, current randomized evidence does not support routine tecovirimat use to accelerate clinical recovery or improve mortality rates in patients with mpox, while further evidence is needed to determine whether selected high-risk populations may benefit from antiviral therapy.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review evaluates the efficacy and safety of tecovirimat for the treatment of mpox virus infection in humans, focusing on clinical outcomes such as time to clinical resolution of skin lesions, lesion resolution by day 28-29, and 28-day all-cause mortality.
Evidence base and method
A systematic review and meta-analysis of randomized controlled trials (RCTs) were conducted, searching PubMed, Embase, and the Cochrane Central Register of Controlled Trials from inception to June 5, 2026. Risk of bias was assessed using RoB 2, and certainty of evidence was evaluated using GRADE. Hazard ratios (HRs) and risk ratios (RRs) were pooled using random-effects models with restricted maximum likelihood estimation and Hartung-Knapp-Sidik-Jonkman adjustment.
Consensus and conclusions
Tecovirimat did not significantly shorten the time to clinical resolution of skin lesions compared with placebo (HR 1.10, 95% CI 0.52-2.31, GRADE: moderate) or increase lesion resolution by day 28-29 (RR 1.00, 95% CI 0.92-1.08, GRADE: high). Virological clearance, pain-related outcomes, and serious adverse events were also comparable between treatment groups.
Controversies and gaps
No statistical heterogeneity was observed (I 2 = 0% for both outcomes). However, mortality at day 28 was rare in both trials, precluding meta-analysis. The review highlights the need for further evidence to determine whether selected high-risk populations may benefit from antiviral therapy.
Research and practice implications
Current randomized evidence does not support routine tecovirimat use to accelerate clinical recovery or improve mortality rates in patients with mpox. Further research is needed to explore its potential benefits in specific high-risk populations.

Journal: The British journal of ophthalmology  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-10-22  |  DOI: 10.1136/bjo-2025-328294  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 110, 11, 1330-1336

猴痘 的进化枝特异性眼科表现:系统评价的总体综述和荟萃分析。

Clade-specific ophthalmic manifestations of mpox: an umbrella review and meta-analysis of systematic reviews.
作者： Oscar Kallay, Luxi Ji, Olalekan A Uthman, Edward J Mills, Alimuddin Zumla, Placide Mbala-Kingebeni, Jean-Jacques Muyembe-Tamfum, Elie Motulsky, Jean B Nachega
摘要中文翻译背景:2024 年 8 月 14 日,世界卫生组织将猴痘 (猴痘) 重新指定为国际关注的突发公共卫生事件。眼部疾病未被充分认识,可能导致不可逆转的视力丧失。澄清其负担、特定分支模式和艾滋病毒相关风险对于临床护理和公共卫生至关重要。方法:我们进行了总体综述和荟萃分析,从成立到 2025 年 9 月系统地检索了 PubMed、Embase、Scopus、Web of Science 和 Cochrane 图书馆。符合条件的研究是报告实验室确诊的 猴痘 病例眼科表现的系统综述,按病毒分支 I、II 和 IIb 分层。使用随机效应荟萃分析和分支分层亚组分析来估计合并比例。该协议已在 PROSPERO 中注册(CRD420251137180)。结果:纳入了五项系统评价(36 项初步研究;28 139 名患者)。结膜炎的汇总比例为 8.9%(95% CI 4.8 至 13.9),在 I 类中最高(21.9%),在 IIb 类中最低(2.7%)。角膜炎(1.5%)、眼睑病变(3.3%)、结膜病变(8.4%)和角膜溃疡(3.3%)因分支而异。总体而言,视力障碍的发生率为 4.5%;仅在进化枝 I 中报告了单侧(0.9%)和双侧(0.4%)失明。根据审查,晚期 HIV 感染者的严重和持续性眼部并发症发生率比 HIV 阴性个体高得多。结论:猴痘 相关眼科疾病可导致严重且可能可预防的视力障碍。与分支 II 相比,分支 I 感染与眼部并发症的负担更大,尤其是 IIb。改善获得眼保健服务的机会、及早识别和整合艾滋病毒和 猴痘 护理对于减少视力丧失至关重要,特别是在资源有限的环境中。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述重点关注 猴痘病毒 的眼科表现,特别检查分支特异性模式以及与 HIV 相关风险的关联。核心问题是确定 猴痘 引起的眼部并发症的负担和特征,重点是 I、II 和 IIb 分支。
证据基础与方法
该综述是系统综述的总括综述和荟萃分析。从成立到 2025 年 9 月,它系统地检索了 PubMed、Embase、Scopus、Web of Science 和 Cochrane 图书馆。符合条件的研究是报告实验室确诊的 猴痘 病例眼科表现的系统评价,按病毒分支 I、II 和 IIb 分层。使用随机效应荟萃分析和分支分层亚组分析来估计合并比例。该协议已在 PROSPERO 中注册(CRD420251137180)。
共识与结论
结膜炎的汇总比例为 8.9%(95% CI 4.8 至 13.9),其中 I 型发病率最高(21.9%),IIb 型发病率最低(2.7%)。角膜炎(1.5%)、眼睑病变(3.3%)、结膜病变(8.4%)和角膜溃疡(3.3%)因进化枝而异。总体而言,视力障碍的发生率为 4.5%,单侧(0.9%)和双侧(0.4%)失明仅报告为 I 型。晚期 HIV 感染者的严重和持续性眼部并发症发生率比 HIV 阴性者高得多。猴痘 相关眼科疾病可导致严重且可能可预防的视力障碍。与分支 II 相比,分支 I 感染与眼部并发症的负担更大,尤其是 IIb。
争议与缺口
该综述强调了不同分支和人群所报告的眼部表现的异质性。虽然注意到了进化枝特定的差异,但证据受到研究数量少和报告中潜在偏差的限制。HIV 与眼部并发症之间的关联得到了支持,但需要进一步研究以确认因果关系。缺乏关于长期视力结果和预防眼部并发症的干预措施有效性的数据。
科研与实践启示
改善眼健康服务的可及性、早期识别眼部症状以及将艾滋病毒和 猴痘 护理相结合对于减少视力丧失至关重要,特别是在资源有限的环境中。需要进一步的研究来探索进化枝特异性眼部表现的机制,并评估有针对性的干预措施在预防和管理这些并发症方面的有效性。

Clade-specific ophthalmic manifestations of mpox: an umbrella review and meta-analysis of systematic reviews.

Authors: Oscar Kallay, Luxi Ji, Olalekan A Uthman, Edward J Mills, Alimuddin Zumla, Placide Mbala-Kingebeni, Jean-Jacques Muyembe-Tamfum, Elie Motulsky, Jean B Nachega
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: On 14 August 2024, the WHO redesignated Monkeypox (mpox) as a Public Health Emergency of International Concern. Ocular disease is under-recognised and can cause irreversible vision loss. Clarifying its burden, clade-specific patterns and HIV-related risks is essential for clinical care and public health. METHODS: We conducted an umbrella review and meta-analysis, systematically searching PubMed, Embase, Scopus, Web of Science and the Cochrane Library from inception to September 2025. Eligible studies were systematic reviews reporting ophthalmic manifestations in laboratory-confirmed mpox cases, stratified by viral clades I, II and IIb. Pooled proportions were estimated using random-effects meta-analysis, with clade-stratified subgroup analyses. The protocol was registered in PROSPERO (CRD420251137180). RESULTS: Five systematic reviews (36 primary studies; 28 139 patients) were included. The pooled proportion of conjunctivitis was 8.9% (95% CI 4.8 to 13.9), highest in clade I (21.9%) and lowest in clade IIb (2.7%). Keratitis (1.5%), eyelid lesions (3.3%), conjunctival lesions (8.4%) and corneal ulceration (3.3%) varied by clade. Visual impairment occurred in 4.5% overall; unilateral (0.9%) and bilateral (0.4%) blindness were reported only with clade I. Across reviews, people living with advanced HIV experienced disproportionately higher rates of severe and persistent ocular complications than HIV-negative individuals. CONCLUSIONS: Mpox-related ophthalmic disease can cause substantial—and potentially preventable—visual disability. Clade I infection is associated with a greater burden of ocular complications than clade II—particularly IIb. Improved access to eye health services, early recognition and integration of HIV and mpox care are critical to reducing vision loss, especially in resource-limited settings.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on the ophthalmic manifestations of mpox virus, specifically examining clade-specific patterns and the association with HIV-related risks. The central question is to determine the burden and characteristics of ocular complications caused by mpox, with an emphasis on clade I, II, and IIb.
Evidence base and method
The review is an umbrella review and meta-analysis of systematic reviews. It systematically searched PubMed, Embase, Scopus, Web of Science, and the Cochrane Library from inception to September 2025. Eligible studies were systematic reviews reporting ophthalmic manifestations in laboratory-confirmed mpox cases, stratified by viral clades I, II, and IIb. Pooled proportions were estimated using random-effects meta-analysis, with clade-stratified subgroup analyses. The protocol was registered in PROSPERO (CRD420251137180).
Consensus and conclusions
The pooled proportion of conjunctivitis was 8.9% (95% CI 4.8 to 13.9), with the highest rates observed in clade I (21.9%) and the lowest in clade IIb (2.7%). Keratitis (1.5%), eyelid lesions (3.3%), conjunctival lesions (8.4%), and corneal ulceration (3.3%) varied by clade. Visual impairment occurred in 4.5% overall, with unilateral (0.9%) and bilateral (0.4%) blindness reported only with clade I. People living with advanced HIV experienced disproportionately higher rates of severe and persistent ocular complications than HIV-negative individuals. Mpox-related ophthalmic disease can cause substantial—and potentially preventable—visual disability. Clade I infection is associated with a greater burden of ocular complications than clade II—particularly IIb.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights heterogeneity in the reported ocular manifestations across clades and populations. While clade-specific differences are noted, the evidence is limited by the small number of studies and potential biases in reporting. The association between HIV and ocular complications is supported but requires further investigation to confirm causality. There is a lack of data on the long-term visual outcomes and the effectiveness of interventions in preventing ocular complications.
Research and practice implications
Improved access to eye health services, early recognition of ocular symptoms, and integration of HIV and mpox care are critical to reducing vision loss, especially in resource-limited settings. Further research is needed to explore the mechanisms underlying clade-specific ocular manifestations and to evaluate the effectiveness of targeted interventions in preventing and managing these complications.

SARS、H1N1、埃博拉、COVID-19、猴痘、H5N1 和汉坦病毒:人类是否正在进入流行病的新时代?土耳其准备好迎接下一个全球 暴发 了吗?

SARS, H1N1, EBOLA, COVID-19, MPOX, H5N1 AND HANTAVIRUS: IS HUMANITY ENTERING A NEW ERA OF PANDEMIC? IS TÜRKİYE PREPARED FOR THE NEXT GLOBAL OUTBREAK?
作者： Sebla Ak
摘要中文翻译传染病的出现和再次出现对全球卫生安全构成持续挑战。暴发,例如严重急性呼吸系统综合症 (SARS)、甲型流感 (H1N1)、埃博拉病毒病、2019 年冠状病毒病 (COVID-19)、猴痘、甲型禽流感 (H5N1) 和汉坦病毒感染,凸显了针对新出现的传染病威胁做好有效准备的必要性。“X疾病”的概念进一步强调了为可能导致未来流行病或大流行的未知病原体做好准备的重要性。本研究旨在检查主要传染病 暴发 并评估土耳其对未来潜在的全球 暴发 的准备情况。基于对科学出版物和机构报告的结构化文献检索和文档分析,采用了描述性综述设计。使用 SWOT 框架对调查结果进行组织和解释。该分析将医疗基础设施、重症监护能力、数字医疗系统以及在 COVID-19 大流行期间获得的经验确定为潜在优势。然而,与医疗保健劳动力能力、人畜共患疾病、公共卫生素养、人类、动物和环境卫生部门之间的协调以及某些生物技术产品对外部来源的依赖相关的挑战可能会限制准备工作。气候变化、环境破坏、国际旅行、移民和全球贸易被认为是可能影响传染病出现和传播的因素。加强综合 监测 和早期预警系统,通过“同一个健康”方法改善部门间协调,提高国内 疫苗 和诊断生产能力,以及扩大基于社区的公共卫生教育,可能有助于改善土耳其对未来 暴发 的准备。这些发现凸显了针对新出现的传染病威胁进行持续、多部门准备的重要性。

SARS, H1N1, EBOLA, COVID-19, MPOX, H5N1 AND HANTAVIRUS: IS HUMANITY ENTERING A NEW ERA OF PANDEMIC? IS TÜRKİYE PREPARED FOR THE NEXT GLOBAL OUTBREAK?

Authors: Sebla Ak
Original abstract (English)
The emergence and re-emergence of infectious diseases pose persistent challenges to global health security. Outbreaks such as severe acute respiratory syndrome (SARS), influenza A(H1N1), Ebola virus disease, coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19), mpox, avian influenza A(H5N1), and hantavirus infections highlight the need for effective preparedness against emerging infectious threats. The concept of “Disease X” further emphasizes the importance of preparing for unknown pathogens that may cause future epidemics or pandemics. This study aimed to examine major infectious disease outbreaks and assess Türkiye’s preparedness for potential future global outbreaks. A descriptive review design was employed, based on a structured literature search and document analysis of scientific publications and institutional reports. The findings were organized and interpreted using a SWOT framework. The analysis identified healthcare infrastructure, intensive care capacity, digital health systems, and experience gained during the COVID-19 pandemic as potential strengths. However, challenges related to healthcare workforce capacity, zoonotic disease surveillance, public health literacy, coordination across human, animal, and environmental health sectors, and dependence on external sources for certain biotechnological products may constrain preparedness. Climate change, environmental disruption, international travel, migration, and global trade were identified as factors that may influence the emergence and spread of infectious diseases. Strengthening integrated surveillance and early warning systems, improving intersectoral coordination through the One Health approach, enhancing domestic vaccine and diagnostic production capacity, and expanding community-based public health education may help improve Türkiye’s preparedness for future outbreaks. These findings highlight the importance of sustained, multisectoral preparedness for emerging infectious disease threats.

Strengthening integrated surveillance and early warning systems, improving intersectoral coordination through the One Health approach, enhancing domestic vaccine and diagnostic production capacity, and expanding community-based public health education may help improve Türkiye’s preparedness for future outbreaks.

撒哈拉以南非洲地区医护人员应对猴痘大流行的准备情况:快速回顾调查知识差距

Healthcare Workers’ Preparedness for Monkeypox Pandemic Response in SubSaharan Africa: A Rapid Review Investigating the Knowledge Gap
作者： Arole Darwin Touko, Ruth Tabi Amin, Gordon Takop Nchanji
摘要中文翻译背景:猴痘 是一种人畜共患病毒性疾病,由撒哈拉以南非洲 (SSA) 流行的 正痘病毒 引起,该病的死灰复燃凸显了改善卫生保健工作者 (HCW) 准备情况的迫切需要。尽管“同一个健康”方法具有潜力,但目前的实施仍然分散。本快速审查综合了 HCW 的知识、态度、信息来源以及 SSA 中 猴痘 准备障碍的证据,以确定培训需求和政策影响。方法:按照系统评价和荟萃分析的首选报告项目 (PRISMA) 指南,使用 MeSH 术语和布尔运算符对 2000 年至 2024 年间发表的研究进行 PubMed 和 ScienceDirect 搜索。纳入了评估 HCW 知识、态度或 SSA 中 猴痘 准备情况的研究,并提供英文或法文全文;那些涉及非医护人员人群、在 SSA 之外进行或缺乏主要数据的研究被排除在外。两名评审员使用标准化模板独立筛选和提取数据;使用 JBI 分析横断面研究批判性评估清单评估质量。结果:纳入了来自 388 份记录的五项研究(喀麦隆、埃塞俄比亚、尼日利亚、刚果民主共和国;n = 1,672 名医护人员)。只有 46.8% 的医护人员表现出足够的 猴痘 知识。障碍包括对社交媒体的依赖和获得正规培训的机会有限。对预防的积极态度与较高的知识水平相关。结论:尽管该地区的病死率接近全球平均水平的十倍,但只有不到一半的 SSA 医护人员表现出足够的 猴痘 知识;积极态度并未转化为临床准备,反映了严重的意向与行动差距;社交媒体作为信息来源占据主导地位,强调了在同一个健康培训框架内需要经过同行评审的替代方案。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
猴痘 是一种人畜共患病毒性疾病,由撒哈拉以南非洲 (SSA) 流行的 正痘病毒 引起,该病的死灰复燃凸显了卫生保健工作者 (HCW) 迫切需要加强做好准备。
证据基础与方法
遵循系统评价和荟萃分析的首选报告项目 (PRISMA) 指南,使用 MeSH 术语和布尔运算符对 2000 年至 2024 年间发表的研究进行 PubMed 和 ScienceDirect 搜索。纳入了评估 HCW 知识、态度或 SSA 中 猴痘 准备情况的研究,并提供英文或法文全文;那些涉及非医护人员人群、在 SSA 之外进行或缺乏主要数据的研究被排除在外。两名评审员使用标准化模板独立筛选和提取数据;使用 JBI 分析横断面研究批判性评估清单评估质量。
共识与结论
纳入了来自 388 份记录的五项研究(喀麦隆、埃塞俄比亚、尼日利亚、刚果民主共和国;n = 1,672 名医护人员)。只有 46.8% 的医护人员表现出足够的 猴痘 知识。障碍包括对社交媒体的依赖和获得正规培训的机会有限。对预防的积极态度与较高的知识水平相关。
争议与缺口
尽管该地区的病死率接近全球平均水平的十倍,但只有不到一半的 SSA 医护人员表现出足够的 猴痘 知识;积极态度并未转化为临床准备,反映了严重的意向与行动差距;社交媒体作为信息来源占据主导地位,强调了在同一个健康培训框架内需要经过同行评审的替代方案。
科研与实践启示
本快速审查综合了 HCW 的知识、态度、信息来源以及 SSA 中 猴痘 准备障碍的证据,以确定培训需求和政策影响。

Healthcare Workers’ Preparedness for Monkeypox Pandemic Response in SubSaharan Africa: A Rapid Review Investigating the Knowledge Gap

Authors: Arole Darwin Touko, Ruth Tabi Amin, Gordon Takop Nchanji
Original abstract (English)
Background: Mpox is a zoonotic viral disease caused by an Orthopoxvirus endemic to sub-Saharan Africa (SSA), where its resurgence underscores the urgent need for improved preparedness among health care workers (HCWs). Despite the potential of a One Health approach, current implementations remain fragmented. This rapid review synthesises evidence on HCWs' knowledge, attitudes, information sources, and barriers to preparedness regarding Mpox in SSA to identify training needs and policy implications. Methods: Following Preferred Reporting Items for Systematic Reviews and Meta-Analyses (PRISMA) guidelines, PubMed and ScienceDirect were searched using MeSH terms and Boolean operators for studies published between 2000 and 2024. Studies assessing HCW knowledge, attitudes, or preparedness for Mpox in SSA with full-text availability in English or French were included; those involving non-HCW populations, conducted outside SSA, or lacking primary data were excluded. Two reviewers independently screened and extracted data using a standardised template; quality was assessed using the JBI Critical Appraisal Checklist for Analytical CrossSectional Studies. Results: Five studies from 388 records were included (Cameroon, Ethiopia, Nigeria, DRC; n = 1,672 HCWs). Only 46.8% of HCWs demonstrated adequate Mpox knowledge. Barriers included reliance on social media and limited access to formal training. Positive attitudes towards prevention correlated with higher knowledge levels. Conclusion: Fewer than half of SSA HCWs demonstrate adequate Mpox knowledge despite a regional case fatality rate nearly ten times the global average; positive attitudes are not translating into clinical preparedness, reflecting a critical intention-action gap; and social media dominates as an information source, underscoring the need for peer-reviewed alternatives within a One Health training framework.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Mpox is a zoonotic viral disease caused by an Orthopoxvirus endemic to sub-Saharan Africa (SSA), where its resurgence underscores the urgent need for improved preparedness among health care workers (HCWs).
Evidence base and method
Following Preferred Reporting Items for Systematic Reviews and Meta-Analyses (PRISMA) guidelines, PubMed and ScienceDirect were searched using MeSH terms and Boolean operators for studies published between 2000 and 2024. Studies assessing HCW knowledge, attitudes, or preparedness for Mpox in SSA with full-text availability in English or French were included; those involving non-HCW populations, conducted outside SSA, or lacking primary data were excluded. Two reviewers independently screened and extracted data using a standardised template; quality was assessed using the JBI Critical Appraisal Checklist for Analytical Cross-Sectional Studies.
Consensus and conclusions
Five studies from 388 records were included (Cameroon, Ethiopia, Nigeria, DRC; n = 1,672 HCWs). Only 46.8% of HCWs demonstrated adequate Mpox knowledge. Barriers included reliance on social media and limited access to formal training. Positive attitudes towards prevention correlated with higher knowledge levels.
Controversies and gaps
Fewer than half of SSA HCWs demonstrate adequate Mpox knowledge despite a regional case fatality rate nearly ten times the global average; positive attitudes are not translating into clinical preparedness, reflecting a critical intention-action gap; and social media dominates as an information source, underscoring the need for peer-reviewed alternatives within a One Health training framework.
Research and practice implications
This rapid review synthesises evidence on HCWs' knowledge, attitudes, information sources, and barriers to preparedness regarding Mpox in SSA to identify training needs and policy implications.

卫生区报告克拉克县居民首例 I 型 猴痘 病例 - KSNV

Health District reports first clade I mpox case in Clark County resident - KSNV
新闻简报内华达州南部卫生区 (SNHD) 于 2026 年 10 月 20 日在克拉克县居民中确认了首例 I 进化枝 猴痘 病例。这标志着该地区 猴痘 情况的新发展。该人没有报告过旅行暴露,SNHD 强调 疫苗接种 的重要性以及对暴露者的及时指导。自2026年8月以来,克拉克县已确诊11例第二支猴痘病例,表明近期病例有所增加。卫生区提醒公众,猴痘 继续在社区中传播,并敦促高危人群接种疫苗。JYNNEOS 疫苗 预计可针对进化枝 I 和 II 猴痘 提供防护。SNHD 在其免疫诊所提供 猴痘 疫苗接种。应对措施包括公共卫生指导和对病毒传播的持续调查。
查看新闻五要素
时间
内华达州南部卫生区(SNHD)于 2026 年 10 月 20 日报告了克拉克县首例 I 型分支 猴痘 病例。该文章于同一天发表,当天确认了第一例 I 型分支病例。
地点与对象
该病例在内华达州克拉克县,特别是拉斯维加斯地区报道。受影响的人是克拉克县的居民,内华达州南部卫生区 (SNHD) 是涉及的当地卫生当局。
事件
内华达州南部卫生区 (SNHD) 确认了克拉克县居民中首例 I 猴痘 分支病例。这是根据卫生区报告确认的事件。
规模、影响与风险
这是克拉克县首例报告的 I 型 猴痘 分支病例。自2026年8月以来,克拉克县已有11例确诊的进化枝II 猴痘病例,表明近期猴痘病例有所增加。该风险被明确指出为病毒在社区中持续传播。
应对、状态与不确定性
内华达州南部卫生区 (SNHD) 鼓励有风险的人接种疫苗,并建议接触者立即寻求指导。JYNNEOS 疫苗 预计可针对进化枝 I 和 II 猴痘 提供防护。回应正在进行中,状态正在调查中。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Health District reports first clade I mpox case in Clark County resident - KSNV

News Brief
The Southern Nevada Health District (SNHD) confirmed the first case of clade I mpox in a Clark County resident on October 20, 2026. This marks a new development in the mpox situation in the area. The individual has no reported travel exposure, and the SNHD is emphasizing the importance of vaccination and prompt guidance for those exposed. Since August 2026, there have been 11 confirmed cases of clade II mpox in Clark County, indicating a recent increase in cases. The health district is reminding the public that mpox continues to circulate in the community and urging people at risk to get vaccinated. The JYNNEOS vaccine is expected to protect against both clade I and II mpox. The SNHD is offering mpox vaccination at its immunization clinics. The response includes public health guidance and ongoing investigation into the spread of the virus.

WATCHREAD MORE NEWS Health District reports first clade I mpox case in Clark County resident by Matthew Seeman Tue, October 20, 2026 at 9:08 AM Updated Tue, October 20, 2026 at 2:53 PM 3VIEW ALL PHOTOS FILE: Southern Nevada Health District (KSNV) 0Comment Share topics: Mpox Clade I Clark County Southern Nevada Health District JYNNEOS Clade II Vaccination Public health LAS VEGAS (KSNV) — Health officials are reporting the first case of clade I mpox in a Clark County resident. The Southern Nevada Health District (SNHD) says the individual has no reported travel exposure. Separately, there have been 11 confirmed cases of clade II mpox in Clark County since this August, marking a recent increase in cases. "This first clade I case and the recent increase in clade II cases are reminders that mpox continues to circulate in our community," Dr. Cassius Lockett, SNHD's District Health Officer, said in a statement. "We encourage people at risk to get vaccinated and anyone who may have been exposed to seek guidance promptly. Recognizing symptoms and avoiding close contact when sick can also help prevent further spread." Clade I and clade II are two types of viruses that cause mpox. Clade II caused the global outbreak that began in 2022 and continues to circulate at low levels in the U.S. Powered By 10 Sec Teen critically injured in Henderson e-scooter crash dies, police continue investigation Next Stay A large clade I outbreak spread widely in Central and Eastern Africa in 2024, with cases later identified in other countries, including the U.S. The JYNNEOS vaccine is expected to protect against both types, and the SNHD offers mpox vaccination at its immunization clinics. Mpox spreads primarily through close, sustained physical contact, including sexual or intimate contact. It can spread through direct contact with a person's rash, sores, scabs or bodily fluids, including during kissing or oral, anal or vaginal sex. It can also spread through contact with contaminated items, such as clothing, bedding or towels, and during pregnancy or childbirth. Symptoms usually begin within three weeks of exposure, and illness usually lasts two to four weeks. A rash may appear as bumps or blisters on or near the genitals or anus, or on other areas such as the hands, feet, chest, face or mouth. Other symptoms can include fever, chills, swollen lymph nodes, headache, muscle aches and fatigue. Some people develop a rash without other symptoms. People with suspected or confirmed mpox should take the following steps to prevent spreading the illness: Stay home and avoid close contact with others, including people and pets in the same household. Avoid sexual contact, kissing and other close physical contact. If being around others is unavoidable, wear a well-fitting mask and completely cover rashes and sores with clothing or bandages. Avoid sharing clothing, bedding, towels and other personal items. Wash hands frequently with soap and water, especially after touching the rash. Use an alcohol-based hand sanitizer if soap and water are unavailable. Time
The Southern Nevada Health District (SNHD) reported the first clade I mpox case in Clark County on October 20, 2026. The article was published on the same date, and the first clade I case was confirmed on that day.
Location and population
The case was reported in Clark County, Nevada, specifically in the Las Vegas area. The affected individual is a resident of Clark County, and the Southern Nevada Health District (SNHD) is the local health authority involved.
Event
The Southern Nevada Health District (SNHD) confirmed the first case of clade I mpox in a Clark County resident. This is a confirmed event based on the health district's report.
Scale, impact and risk
This is the first reported case of clade I mpox in Clark County. Since August 2026, there have been 11 confirmed cases of clade II mpox in Clark County, indicating a recent increase in mpox cases. The risk is explicitly stated as ongoing circulation of the virus in the community.

Clark County Confirms First Clade I Mpox Case Without Reported Travel Exposure - Hoodline

News Brief
Clark County, Nevada has confirmed its first case of clade I mpox without any reported travel exposure. The Southern Nevada Health District reported the case, marking a new development in the local mpox outbreak. Health officials are urging people at risk to get vaccinated and those exposed to seek guidance promptly. No deaths or exposures have been reported, and the source of transmission remains unclear.

The Southern Nevada Health District confirmed the case this week, marking the first time the clade I strain has turned up in a local resident without a clear travel link. The health district reported the case as part of a broader update on mpox activity in the Las Vegas area, according to KSNV. Since the virus was first detected locally in June 2022, Clark County has logged 365 total mpox cases, according to the Southern Nevada Health District. The district also noted that 11 confirmed clade II cases have surfaced locally between August and October 2026, a sign of increased virus activity heading into the clade I discovery. No Clear Source of Exposure SNHD has said the individual diagnosed with clade I mpox has no reported travel exposure, leaving the source of transmission unclear. Dr. Cassius Lockett, the district's health officer, said mpox continues to circulate in the community and encouraged people at risk to get vaccinated and those potentially exposed to seek guidance promptly. Lockett also said that recognizing symptoms early and avoiding close contact with others while sick can help prevent further spread, per the same report from the health district. Clade I and clade II are the two viral types behind mpox illness. Clade II caused the global outbreak that began in 2022 and continues to circulate at low levels across the U.S., while clade I spread widely across Central and Eastern Africa in 2024 before cases began appearing in other countries, including the United States, the health district's report notes. Why Clade I Draws Closer Scrutiny The heightened concern around clade I stems from its track record overseas. According to the Johns Hopkins Bloomberg School of Public Health, clade I mpox has historically produced case fatality rates between 1% and 10% in African outbreaks, compared with less than 1% to 4% for clade II. The World Health Organization declared clade I a Public Health Emergency of International Concern in August 2024 amid a rapid surge of the clade Ib strain across Central and Eastern Africa, its second such mpox declaration. Clade I's arrival in the U.S. has followed a steady progression. The first known American case was confirmed in a traveler returning from Eastern Africa to California in November 2024, according to the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention. Nearly a year later, in October 2025, Long Beach, California recorded the nation's first locally acquired clade I case in a patient with no travel history, as reported by CIDRAP. Similar travel-linked and non-travel cases have since surfaced elsewhere, including in Arizona and San Diego County. Vaccine and Exposure Guidance Health officials emphasize that the Jynneos vaccine is expected to protect against both clade I and clade II mpox, and SNHD continues to offer it at its immunization clinics. By August 2024, clinics affiliated with the health district had already administered more than 11,740 doses across Clark County, reflecting the scale of earlier vaccination efforts following the 2022 outbreak. The standard Jynneos regimen for adults 18 and older requires two doses spaced four weeks, or 28 days, apart to achieve full protection, according to the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention. For those who may have been exposed, the agency says post-exposure vaccination works best within four days of exposure, though getting the shot between four and 14 days afterward can still reduce how severe symptoms become. Anyone potentially exposed should monitor for symptoms for 21 days following their last contact, per SNHD guidance. How Mpox Spreads and What Symptoms Look Like Mpox spreads primarily through close, sustained physical contact, including direct contact with rash, sores, scabs or bodily fluids, as well as through kissing or oral, anal or vaginal sex. It can also spread through contaminated clothing, bedding or towels, and through pregnancy or childbirth. Symptoms usually begin within three weeks of exposure and the illness typically lasts two to four weeks, with a rash that may appear on or near the genitals or anus, or on the hands, feet, chest, face or mouth. Other symptoms can include fever, chills, swollen lymph nodes, headache, muscle aches and fatigue, though some people develop only a rash without any other symptoms. Anyone with suspected or confirmed mpox is advised to stay home and avoid close contact with others, including household members and pets, and to avoid sexual contact, kissing and other close physical contact. If contact with others is unavoidable, health officials recommend wearing a well-fitting mask and completely covering rashes and sores with clothing or bandages. People with mpox should also avoid sharing clothing, bedding, towels and other personal items, and should wash their hands frequently with soap and water, especially after touching the rash, using an alcohol-based hand sanitizer when soap and water aren't available. Isolation should continue until all sores have healed, scabs have fallen off and a fresh layer of skin has formed, per the health district's guidance. By Rachel Zalucki Published: Oct. 20, 2026 at 3:57 PM UTC Share Add Us On GoogleAdd as a preferred source on Google LAS VEGAS (FOX5) — The Southern Nevada Health District announced Tuesday the first confirmed case of Clade I Mpox in a Clark County resident. The individual has no reported travel exposure, according to the Health District. MORE ON FOX5: More than 37,000 vote in first three days of early voting in Clark County The Health District has also reported 11 confirmed cases of Clade II Mpox since August 2026, marking a recent increase in cases. Since the first mpox case was reported in Clark County in June 2022, the Health District has reported 365 cases. “This first clade I case and the recent increase in clade II cases are reminders that mpox continues to circulate in our community,” said Dr. Cassius Lockett, District Health Officer for the Health District. “We encourage people at risk to get vaccinated and anyone who may have been exposed to seek guidance promptly. Recognizing symptoms and avoiding close contact when sick can also help prevent further spread.” About the two virus types Mpox is caused by two types of virus, known as clade I and clade II, according to the Health District. Clade II caused the global outbreak that began in 2022 and continues to circulate at low levels in the United States. A Clade I outbreak spread widely in Central and Eastern Africa in 2024, with cases subsequently identified in other countries, including the United States. Both types can be prevented in the same ways, and the JYNNEOS vaccine is expected to protect against both, the Health District said. Mpox spreads primarily through close, sustained physical contact, including sexual or intimate contact, according to the Health District. It can spread through direct contact with a person’s rash, sores, scabs or bodily fluids, including during kissing or oral, anal or vaginal sex. It can also spread through contact with contaminated items, such as clothing, bedding or towels, and during pregnancy or childbirth. Symptoms typically begin within three weeks of exposure, and illness usually lasts two to four weeks, the Health District said. A rash may appear as bumps or blisters on or near the genitals or anus, or on other areas such as the hands, feet, chest, face or mouth. Other symptoms can include fever, chills, swollen lymph nodes, headache, muscle aches and fatigue. Some people develop a rash without other symptoms. Anyone who develops a new or unexplained rash or other symptoms consistent with mpox should avoid close contact with others and contact a health care provider, according to the Health District. People are advised to call ahead before visiting a health care facility to let staff know about possible mpox symptoms. The Health District offers mpox vaccination for eligible people ages 18 and older at its immunization clinics. The vaccine is given in two doses, four weeks apart. Appointments are available on the Southern Nevada Health District website. Copyright 2026 KVVU. All rights reserved. Clark County confirms first case of clade I monkeypox virus - KTNV Las Vegas
新闻简报据拉斯维加斯 KTNV 报道,内华达州克拉克县于 2026 年 10 月 25 日确认了第一例 I 型猴痘病毒病例。报告称,该病例是通过官方卫生渠道发现的,目前尚未报告其他病例、死亡或接触情况。卫生专家敦促当地人保持警惕,并密切关注事态发展。调查仍在进行中,尚未披露有关病毒来源或传播的更多细节。这一确认凸显了持续提高公共卫生意识和做好准备的必要性,特别是考虑到与 I 型猴痘相关的潜在风险。报告没有提及现阶段任何具体的预防措施或遏制策略。

B routine priorityPublic-health news

Clark County confirms first case of clade I monkeypox virus - KTNV Las Vegas

News Brief
Clark County, Nevada, confirmed the first case of clade I monkeypox virus on October 25, 2026, according to KTNV Las Vegas. The report states that the case was identified through official health channels, and no additional cases, deaths, or exposures have been reported at this time. Health experts are urging locals to remain vigilant and are monitoring the situation closely. The investigation is ongoing, and no further details on the source or spread of the virus have been disclosed. This confirmation highlights the need for continued public health awareness and preparedness, especially given the potential risk associated with clade I monkeypox. The report does not mention any specific prevention measures or containment strategies at this stage.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

KP 发现 4 起 猴痘 案件

Four Mpox cases detected in KP
新闻简报2026 年 10 月 25 日,开伯尔-普赫图赫瓦省 (KP) 当局报告了 4 例新的 猴痘 病例,其中包括一对没有出国旅行史的夫妇。这些病例是通过开伯尔医科大学公共卫生参考实验室的检测得到证实的。该省今年的 猴痘 人数达到 18 人,死亡人数为 1 人。卫生专家担心当地 传播 的风险,并敦促采取更强有力的预防措施,例如感染控制方案和医院隔离室。官员们已开始对感染者的近亲进行接触者追踪和检测。据了解,猴痘病毒 通过直接或间接接触受感染的患者或受污染的材料进行传播。此前,开伯尔省的大多数病例都与国际旅行有关,但目前的 暴发 引起了社区 暴发 的担忧。医生强调防止与受影响人群直接接触并采取预防措施降低感染风险的重要性。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该新闻报道于 2026 年 10 月 25 日发布,四例 猴痘 病例是在该报告发布前一周发现的。这些病例于周六(很可能是 2026 年 10 月 22 日)检测出 猴痘 呈阳性。
地点与对象
这些病例是在开伯尔-普赫图赫瓦省(KP)发现的,特别是在瑙谢拉、白沙瓦和马尔丹。受影响的人群包括来自这些地区的个人,没有出国旅行史的报告。
事件
在开普敦发现了四例新的 猴痘 病例,其中包括一对夫妇,卫生官员计划对接触者进行追踪并对近亲进行检测,以预防当地的 猴痘 病例。这些病例是通过开伯尔医科大学公共卫生参考实验室的实验室检测得到确认的。
规模、影响与风险
该省今年的 猴痘 病例总数达到 18 例,其中 1 人死亡。卫生专家对当地传播的风险表示担忧,并强调需要采取更有力的预防措施。该疾病可通过直接或间接接触感染者或受污染的材料传播。
应对、状态与不确定性
卫生官员已开始对接触者进行追踪并对近亲进行检测。已向受影响地区的医院发出实验室确认和严格监测的指南。这种情况令人震惊,专家呼吁采取感染控制措施以防止进一步传播。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Four Mpox cases detected in KP

News Brief
On October 25, 2026, authorities in Khyber Pakhtunkhwa (KP) reported four new Mpox cases, including a couple, with no history of foreign travel. These cases were confirmed through testing at the Public Health Reference Laboratory at Khyber Medical University. The province's Mpox tally for the year reached 18, with one death recorded. Health experts are concerned about the risk of local transmission and have urged stronger preventive measures, such as infection control protocols and isolation rooms in hospitals. Officials have started contact tracing and testing of close relatives of the infected individuals. The Mpox virus is known to spread through direct or indirect contact with infected patients or contaminated materials. Previously, most cases in KP were linked to international travel, but the current outbreak raises fears of community transmission. Doctors emphasize the importance of preventing direct contact with affected people and taking precautionary steps to reduce the risk of infection.
Original source text (English)
EPAPER LIVE TV DAWNNEWS URDU Images Herald Aurora CityFM89 Advertise Events Supplements Careers Obituaries Dawn E-Paper | October 26, 2026 Home Latest US-Iran War Pakistan Opinion Business Images Prism World Sport Breathe Magazines Tech Videos Popular Archive Flood Donations Home Latest US-Iran War Pakistan Opinion Business Images Prism World Sport Breathe Magazines Tech Videos Popular Archive Flood Donations Search Search Subscribe to notifications Get the latest news and updates from Dawn Not Now Allow Notifications Four Mpox cases detected in KP Ashfaq Yusufzai Published October 25, 2026 Updated October 25, 2026 10:27am Email Story Your Name * Recipient Email * Send Email Cancel 0 A test tube labelled "Mpox virus positive" is held in this illustration taken August 20, 2024. — Reuters/File comments Join our Whatsapp Channel PESHAWAR: Authorities have detected four new Mpox cases, including a couple, in Khyber Pakhtunkhwa, revealing that none of them has a history of foreign travel. As health experts fear the risk of the local transmission of the virus, the health department has planned contact tracing and testing of the close relatives of the infected people, say officials. They said the Public Health Reference Laboratory at the Khyber Medical University, which tested samples for infectious diseases, received samples from suspected patients referred by the Khyber Teaching Hospital last week. They tested positive for Mpox on Saturday. Officials said one woman and three men were sent home to remain in isolation. Contact tracing, testing planned amid fears of virus’s local transmission They said two patients belonged to Nowshera, while the other two were from Peshawar and Mardan. According to a report prepared by the health department’s Integrated Disease Surveillance and Response System, the latest case detections have taken the province’s Mpox tally for the current year to 18. Of them, one lost his life, while 13 patients were recovered. Doctors called the situation alarming in light of the possibility of community transmission. Until now this year, the province has reported two Mpox cases in Swabi in Jan; four in Feb, including two in Buner and one each in Abbottabad and Lower Chitral; one in Peshawar in March; one in Karak in April; one in Upper Chitral in July; one in Mardan in Aug, and four in Sept, including three in Peshawar and one in Upper Dir. Doctors fear outbreaks and call for wider infection prevention measures along with the establishment of isolation rooms in hospitals to help prevent the spread of the disease. Health officials said the department had already issued guidelines to hospitals, especially those in affected districts, asking them to collect samples from suspected Mpox patients for laboratory confirmation to ensure strict surveillance through timely diagnosis. They said Mpox cases reported in the province previously were mainly linked to international travel, with patients contracting the infection abroad before entering the country. Officials said the province recorded its first Mpox case in 2023 when a resident working as a driver in Saudi Arabia tested positive for the disease after landing at the airport. Health experts called stronger preventive measures to contain possible local transmission of the disease. “We need to strengthen infection control measures and ensure that the reuse of syringes is stopped. More infections could be detected if screening is carried out in areas from where the latest cases have been reported,” an expert told Dawn. Dermatologists noted that the World Health Organisation declared Mpox a public health emergency of international concern on Aug 14, 2024, following an upsurge in cases amid the continuing threat posed by the infection. Prof Mehran Khan, associate professor and chairman of the dermatology department at Khyber Teaching Hospital, said the disease typically began with fever, headache, muscle aches, back pain, swollen lymph nodes, chills and exhaustion. He said the rash evolved from macules (flat lesions) to papules (raised lesions), vesicles (fluid-filled lesions), pustules (pus-filled lesions) and finally crusts, while the lesions eventually fell off. “Mpox is transmittable through direct contact with blood, bodily fluids or cutaneous or mucosal lesions of infected patients and indirect contact through contaminated materials such as bedding,” he said. Doctors said the risk of infection could be reduced by preventing direct contact with affected people, wearing face masks and taking recommended precautionary steps. They also said in Pakistan, only Clade-I variant of Mpox virus was prevalent but it wasn’t threatening like Clade-II whose presence had so far not been reported in the country. Doctors said the province had recorded one death among Mpox patients though the deceased might have been a “co-morbid” Mpox patient. B routine priorityPublic-health news

Tally hits 95 as two more mpox cases reported in Sindh - The Express Tribune

News Brief
On October 23, 2026, Sindh, Pakistan reported two additional mpox cases, raising the total caseload for the year to 95. The virus has caused 11 deaths so far. The latest cases involve Salman Ali, a 21-year-old, and Ishrat Fatima, a 23-year-old, both of whom tested positive for mpox at the Sindh Infectious Diseases Hospital. Of the 95 cases, 71 were men and 24 were women, with the 30 to 39 age group being the most affected. Twenty-three children under nine also tested positive. The virus spreads through close physical contact, lesions, bodily fluids, or contaminated objects. Officials emphasized the need for continued surveillance and preventive measures to contain the spread. Both patients are currently receiving treatment at the hospital. The article does not mention any unresolved information or limitations in the source.
Original source text (English)
TODAY’S PAPER | October 26, 2026 | EPAPER ☰ Home Latest Pakistan Business World Sports Opinion Life & Style T-Magazine Blogs Tech Cartoon Home Sindh Tally hits 95 as two more mpox cases reported in Sindh Province reports 11 deaths from virus so far this year Our Correspondent October 23, 2026 1 min read facebook twitter whatsup linkded email The image shows Mpox micrograph in the background. PHOTO: PIXABAY KARACHI: Sindh has reported two more cases of monkeypox, or mpox, taking the total caseload recorded in the province this year to 95. The virus has claimed 11 lives so far. The latest cases involve 21-year-old Salman Ali and 23-year-old Ishrat Fatima, both of whom tested positive for the virus, according to a report by Dow University's provincial public health laboratory. Advertisement X Both patients are already receiving treatment at the Sindh Infectious Diseases Hospital, officials said. The emergence of additional cases has underscored the importance of continued surveillance and preventive measures to contain the spread of the virus. Of the 95 cases thus far, 71 were men, accounting for 75.27 per cent, while 24 were women. People aged 30 to 39 were the most affected age group, with 26 confirmed cases, while 23 children up to the age of nine also tested positive for mpox. April recorded the highest number of cases at 31, followed by September with 20, August 17, July seven, October six so far, March five, June four and May two. Experts said mpox is a viral disease that can spread through close physical or skin-to-skin contact with an infected person, as well as contact with lesions, bodily fluids or contaminated objects. Suspected patients should also limit close contact with others to reduce the risk of transmission, experts said. Event
Two additional mpox cases were reported in Sindh, bringing the total caseload for the year to 95. The latest cases involve Salman Ali and Ishrat Fatima, both of whom tested positive for mpox at the Sindh Infectious Diseases Hospital.
Scale, impact and risk
A total of 95 mpox cases have been reported in Sindh this year, with 11 deaths. The cases highlight the need for continued surveillance and preventive measures to contain the spread of the virus. The virus spreads through close physical contact, lesions, bodily fluids, or contaminated objects.

Officials stated that both patients are receiving treatment at the Sindh Infectious Diseases Hospital. The emergence of additional cases has emphasized the importance of surveillance and preventive measures. The article does not mention any unresolved information or limitations in the source.

Sindh reports two more monkeypox cases, tally rises to 98 - The Express Tribune

News Brief
The Sindh Health Department reported two additional monkeypox cases on October 24, 2026, bringing the provincial total to 98. This marks an intensification of the outbreak, with cases now being reported among Karachi residents who had no history of foreign travel. The department has stepped up surveillance, contact tracing, and patient care, emphasizing the importance of early testing, isolation, and strict infection prevention measures in healthcare facilities. Authorities have also advised the public to maintain proper hand hygiene and avoid direct contact with rashes, blisters, or sores on suspected or confirmed patients. Citizens are urged to seek immediate medical attention if they develop unusual rashes or blisters and to inform their doctors about any contact with a suspected monkeypox patient. The health department reiterated that monkeypox is a preventable and treatable public health concern, urging reliance on official sources for verified information.

The health department said mpox was a preventable and treatable public health concern, urging citizens to follow precautionary measures and rely on official sources for verified information rather than spreading unconfirmed reports. Sindh's mpox outbreak has intensified in recent months, with health authorities reporting a steady rise in infections across the province. Earlier this month, The Express Tribune reported 95 confirmed cases and 11 deaths, highlighting a growing need for stronger surveillance, early diagnosis and contact tracing. The outbreak has extended beyond imported infections, with cases reported among Karachi residents who had no history of foreign travel. In June, a 26-year-old security guard in Karachi had tested positive, taking the provincial tally to 37 at the time. Earlier reports also documented infections in Khairpur and Karachi, prompting authorities to establish isolation facilities and strengthen infection-control arrangements in hospitals. Health experts have warned that delayed diagnosis and stigma can discourage patients from seeking timely medical care, increasing the risk of further transmission. Mpox can spread through close physical contact with an infected person, contact with skin lesions and contaminated personal items. Symptoms can include fever, body aches, swollen lymph nodes, and unusual skin rashes or blisters. B routine priorityPublic-health news

Mpox virus claims life of one more child - The Express Tribune

News Brief
A child has died of mpox in Sindh, Pakistan, raising the province's death toll to 12 this year. The death was confirmed by a spokesperson from the Sindh Infectious Diseases Hospital at NIPA, who stated the child had been transferred from a private hospital a few days earlier. Health authorities have intensified surveillance, contact tracing, and treatment measures following seven new mpox cases reported over the past week. The total number of confirmed mpox cases in Sindh has reached 98. The virus is contagious and spreads through close physical contact, particularly via skin rashes, blisters, lesions, and contaminated personal belongings. Vulnerable groups include infants, individuals with weak immune systems, and those with other serious medical conditions. Authorities emphasize the importance of early diagnosis, isolation of infected patients, and strict adherence to medical guidelines to prevent further transmission. The report is based on a full original article published by The Express Tribune on October 25, 2026.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

CDC数据显示猴痘病例创两年新高 - 台北时报

Mpox cases hit two-year high, CDC data show - Taipei Times
新闻简报The Taipei Times reported that mpox cases in Taiwan have reached a two-year high, with 18 new cases reported this month as of Monday, according to CDC data. Local cases have been reported weekly for seven consecutive weeks, and a total of 52 cases were recorded this year, comprising 41 locally acquired and 11 imported. The CDC highlighted that 16 of the 18 cases were linked to unsafe sex, with only two having received the mpox vaccine. The risk of cross-border transmission of Clade 1b mpox is increasing, as 68 countries and areas have confirmed cases. The WHO assessment report from August indicated a moderate global public health risk for individuals with multiple sexual partners. The CDC urged eligible individuals to get vaccinated, noting that the full two-dose regimen provides about 90 percent protection, while one dose offers 40 to 80 percent. The agency also emphasized that people engaging in high-risk sexual behavior should consider vaccination to prevent mpox infection.
查看新闻五要素
时间
As of Monday, 18 new cases of mpox had been reported this month, with local cases having been reported weekly for seven consecutive weeks. The 52 mpox cases reported so far this year is about 20 percent higher than the same period last year. The risk of cross-border Clade 1b mpox viral transmission is increasing, with 68 countries and areas having reported confirmed cases.
地点与对象
台湾今年迄今报告了 52 例 猴痘 病例,其中 41 例本地感染病例和 11 例输入病例。本地病例包括去年同期的22例,受影响人群包括有多个性伴侣者、性工作者和在商业场所发生性行为的人。
事件
台湾猴痘病例创两年来新高,本月新增18例,本土病例连续7周每周报告。CDC 报告称,18 例病例中有 16 例与不安全性行为有关,只有 2 例接受了 猴痘 疫苗。CDC还指出,跨境传播的风险正在增加,已有68个国家和地区出现确诊病例。
规模、影响与风险
截至周一,本月新增 猴痘 病例 18 例,今年累计病例 52 例。其中,41架是本地采购的,11架是进口的,其中6架来自泰国,2架来自香港。CDC表示,跨境Clade 1b 猴痘 传播的风险正在增加,世界卫生组织的评估报告表明,对于拥有多个性伴侣的个人来说,存在中等的全球公共卫生风险。
应对、状态与不确定性
The CDC is conducting contact tracing and urging eligible individuals to get vaccinated. Vaccination is highlighted as the most effective method of prevention, with the full two-dose regimen providing about 90 percent protection. The CDC also emphasized that people who have engaged in high-risk sexual behavior should consider vaccination.

Front Page Taiwan News Business Editorial & Opinion Sports World News Features Bilingual Pages Search Search All Front Page Taiwan News Business Editorial & Opinion Sports World News Features Bilingual Pages September 26, 2026 - October 25, 2026 GO Home Taiwan News Thu, Oct 22, 2026 page4 Mpox cases hit two-year high, CDC data show By Lee I-chia / Staff reporter Add TT as Preferred Source As of Monday, 18 new cases of mpox had been reported this month, topping the highest monthly number in two years, with local cases having been reported weekly for seven consecutive weeks, the Centers for Disease Control (CDC) said on Tuesday. CDC data showed that 52 mpox cases had been reported so far this year as of Monday, comprising 41 locally acquired cases and 11 imported. CDC Epidemic Intelligence Center Director Guo Hung-wei (郭宏偉) said 18 cases were reported in the first 19 days of this month alone, including 16 with the Clade 1b mpox virus, a variant first identified in the Democratic Republic of the Congo in September 2023. Centers for Disease Control Deputy Director-General and spokesman Lin Min-cheng speaks at a news conference in Taipei in an undated photograph. Photo: Lo Pi, Taipei Times Contact tracing showed that the 16 cases were aged between 20 and 50, with all of them having had unsafe sex and only two having received an mpox vaccine, Guo said. Thirty people have tested positive for Clade 1b mpox so far this year, with 22 local cases and eight imported — six from Thailand and two from Hong Kong, he said. The risk of cross-border Clade 1b mpox viral transmission is increasing, with 68 countries and areas having reported confirmed cases, while mpox cases have been rising in parts of the WHO’s Western Pacific Region, Guo said. A WHO assessment report published in August showed that overall global public health risk is “moderate” for “individuals with multiple sexual partners,” and “low” for all other individuals, he said. CDC Deputy Director-General and spokesman Lin Min-cheng (林明誠) said that local mpox cases have been reported weekly for seven consecutive weeks. The 52 local mpox cases reported so far this year is about 20 percent higher than the same period last year, Lin said. “We continue to urge people who are eligible for [mpox] vaccination to get fully vaccinated with two doses for full protection,” he said. Vaccination is the most effective method of preventing mpox infection, the CDC said, adding that getting the full two-dose regimen provides about 90 percent protection, while one dose provides about 40 to 80 percent. There are 327 healthcare facilities across Taiwan that provide publicly funded mpox vaccinations, so people who have engaged in high-risk sexual behavior, such as having multiple sexual partners, engaged in sex work or had sex at commercial venues should get vaccinated, it said. The storm was moving west-northwest at 23kph and was expected to strengthen over the next 24 hours before weakening slightly as it approaches cooler waters east of Taiwan, Tseng said. Location and population
Taiwan, with 52 mpox cases reported so far this year, comprising 41 locally acquired cases and 11 imported. The local cases include 22 from the same period last year, and the affected population includes individuals with multiple sexual partners, sex workers, and those who had sex at commercial venues.
Event
Mpox cases in Taiwan have reached a two-year high, with 18 new cases reported this month and local cases reported weekly for seven consecutive weeks. The CDC reported that 16 of the 18 cases were associated with unsafe sex and only two had received the mpox vaccine. The CDC also noted that the risk of cross-border transmission is increasing, with 68 countries and areas having confirmed cases.
Scale, impact and risk
As of Monday, 18 new mpox cases were reported this month, with 52 cases in total this year. Of these, 41 were locally acquired, and 11 were imported, including six from Thailand and two from Hong Kong. The CDC stated that the risk of cross-border Clade 1b mpox transmission is increasing, and the WHO assessment report indicated a moderate global public health risk for individuals with multiple sexual partners.
Response, status and uncertainty
The CDC is conducting contact tracing and urging eligible individuals to get vaccinated. Vaccination is highlighted as the most effective method of prevention, with the full two-dose regimen providing about 90 percent protection. The CDC also emphasized that people who have engaged in high-risk sexual behavior should consider vaccination.

猴痘 在信德省夺走了另一名儿童的生命,今年的死亡人数上升至 12 人 - 快报论坛报

Mpox claims another child's life in Sindh, this year's death toll rises to 12 - The Express Tribune
新闻简报2026 年 10 月 24 日星期六,信德省卡拉奇市的一名儿童因 猴痘 死亡,使该年死亡人数增加至 12 人。信德省传染病医院证实了这一死亡事件,并指出该患者是从一家私立医院转院而来。卫生当局今年报告了信德省 98 例 猴痘 病例,其中卡拉奇病例数最多,为 26 例。该病毒通过密切接触传播,包括皮肤损伤和受污染的个人物品,对婴儿、免疫功能低下的人和有潜在健康问题的人构成更高的风险。作为回应,出于感染控制考虑,信德省卫生保健委员会封锁了马里尔 15 的伊本·哈桑医院,并指示卫生机构遵守严格的规定。《论坛快报》寻求专家意见,以了解 诊断 的影响,强调需要尽早采取 诊断、隔离和卫生措施,以防止进一步传播。
查看新闻五要素
时间
2026年10月24日(发布日期);活动日期为 2026 年 10 月 24 日星期六(N2)。
地点与对象
信德省、卡拉奇以及信德省其他地区(N1、N2、N19、N20)。
事件
据信德省传染病医院 (N2、N3) 报道,2026 年 10 月 24 日星期六,一名儿童在信德省卡拉奇死于 猴痘。
规模、影响与风险
今年信德省的猴痘死亡人数已上升至12人,总共报告了98例(N2、N4)。该病毒可以通过密切的身体接触、受污染的物品传播,并影响儿童和成人,其中婴儿、免疫功能低下的人和患有基础疾病的人(N6、N14、N16)的风险较高。
应对、状态与不确定性
卫生当局加强了 监测、接触者追踪和 治疗 措施 (N2)。出于感染控制考虑,信德省卫生保健委员会封锁了位于卡拉奇 Malir 15 的伊本·哈桑医院 (N23)。患者正在政府医院接受治疗,医疗机构也被要求遵循感染预防措施 (N27)。《论坛快报》联系了健康专家以获取信息(N5)。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Mpox claims another child's life in Sindh, this year's death toll rises to 12 - The Express Tribune

News Brief
A child in Karachi, Sindh, died from mpox on Saturday, October 24, 2026, raising the death toll for the year to 12. The Sindh Infectious Diseases Hospital confirmed the death, noting the patient had been transferred from a private hospital. Health authorities reported 98 mpox cases in Sindh this year, with Karachi accounting for the highest number at 26. The virus spreads through close contact, including skin lesions and contaminated personal items, and poses a higher risk for infants, immunocompromised individuals, and those with underlying health conditions. In response, the Sindh Healthcare Commission sealed Ibn-e-Hasan Hospital in Malir 15 due to infection-control concerns, and health facilities are being directed to follow strict protocols. The Express Tribune sought expert opinions to understand the implications of the outbreak, emphasizing the need for early diagnosis, isolation, and hygiene measures to prevent further spread.

According to Sindh Infectious Diseases Hospital spokesperson Rashid Khan, the child, a resident of Karachi, was being treated in the hospital in NIPA for monkeypox and passed away during treatment, further revealed that the patient had been transferred to the facility from a private hospital a few days earlier. Khan said the total number of mpox cases reported in Sindh has climbed to 98. The Express Tribune contacted Sindh Institute of Skin Diseases (Regal) Additional Director and consultant dermatologist Dr Abdullah Yahya to learn about the disease. Dr Yahya also emphasised the importance of early diagnosis, isolating infected individuals and following medical advice to prevent further transmission. “Mpox can affect both children and adults, although the severity of the illness varies from person to person. Infants, people with weakened immune systems and those suffering from other underlying medical conditions may face a higher risk of severe illness and complications,” the skin doctor outlined. He also underscored the common symptoms of the virus, saying it includes fever, body aches, swollen lymph nodes, and unusual skin rashes, blisters or sores. According to Dr Yahya, suspected and confirmed patients should be kept separate under medical supervision, with appropriate infection-prevention measures in place. Most patients can recover with appropriate medical care and monitoring, he said, adding that immediate medical attention was necessary if severe symptoms developed. Khairpur has reported 22 cases, followed by Sukkur with nine and Hyderabad with five. Ghotki, Kashmore and Shaheed Benazirabad have recorded three cases each. Hospital sealed over infection-control concerns Separately, the Sindh Healthcare Commission has sealed Ibn-e-Hasan Hospital in Malir 15, Karachi, following concerns over infection-prevention arrangements after a laboratory report confirmed mpox in a 20-day-old newborn. According to information available to The Express Tribune, the infant had undergone circumcision at the hospital. The case has raised concerns about the implementation of infection-control protocols at healthcare facilities, particularly the sterilisation of medical instruments and the use of proper sterile procedures during treatment. Health authorities have stepped up efforts to identify and monitor close contacts of infected patients to prevent further spread of the virus. Patients being treated at government hospitals are being provided with medical care, while public and private healthcare facilities have been directed to follow prescribed infection-prevention measures and use appropriate protective equipment when examining and treating suspected cases. 巴基斯坦信德省一名婴儿死于 猴痘,使 2027 年死亡人数达到 12 人 - 阿拉伯新闻 PK

Infant dies of mpox in Pakistan’s Sindh, taking 2027 tally to 12 - Arab News PK
新闻简报2026 年 10 月 24 日,巴基斯坦卡拉奇的一名婴儿在一家非法医院进行包皮环切术时感染病毒,后死于 猴痘。卫生官员证实了这起死亡事件,将其归因于该设施卫生条件差和感染控制措施不足。这一事件引起了信德当地 猴痘 传播 的担忧,该地区在 2026 年 9 月下旬至 2026 年 10 月期间报告了 19 例病例,其中卡拉奇有 12 例。信德省今年已记录了 98 例 猴痘 病例和 12 例死亡。专家警告说,监管不力的医疗机构中的手术器械可能成为 传播 的来源,敦促加强 监测 和接触者追踪,以遏制 暴发。信德省医疗保健委员会已封锁该医院,理由是需要提高安全标准。尽管病毒的确切传播方式尚不清楚,但该病例凸显了医疗机构感染预防不足的风险。

时间
阿拉伯新闻 PK 阿拉伯新闻 阿拉伯新闻 JP 阿拉伯新闻 FR 周日。

巴基斯坦信德省,特别是卡拉奇的马里尔地区,有一名婴儿受影响。

巴基斯坦卡拉奇,一名婴儿在一家非法医院进行包皮环切手术时感染 猴痘,随后死亡。卫生官员证实了这起死亡事件,并将其与医院卫生和感染控制措施不佳有关。

B routine priorityPublic-health news

Infant dies of mpox in Pakistan’s Sindh, taking 2027 tally to 12 - Arab News PK

News Brief
An infant in Karachi, Pakistan, died of mpox on October 24, 2026, after contracting the virus during a circumcision at an illegal hospital. Health officials confirmed the death, attributing it to poor hygiene and inadequate infection control measures at the facility. This incident has raised concerns about local mpox transmission in Sindh, where 19 cases were reported between late September and October 2026, including 12 in Karachi. Sindh has recorded 98 mpox cases and 12 deaths this year. Experts warn that surgical instruments in underregulated health facilities could become sources of transmission, urging stronger surveillance and contact tracing to contain the outbreak. The Sindh Healthcare Commission has sealed the hospital, citing the need for improved safety standards. While the exact spread of the virus remains unclear, the case underscores the risks of inadequate infection prevention in healthcare settings.
Original source text (English)
Arab News PK Arab News Arab News JP Arab News FR Sunday . October 25, 2026 Pakistan Saudi Arabia Middle East World Business Opinion Lifestyle Sport Latest News/Pakistan Pakistan says 2 soldiers, 6 militants killed during counterterror operation in northwest Home Infant dies of mpox in Pakistan’s Sindh, taking 2027 tally to 12 Infant dies of mpox in Pakistan’s Sindh, taking 2027 tally to 12 A poster indicating an isolation ward, prepared for mpox patients, is seen at the Police and Services hospital in Peshawar on August 20, 2024. (AFP/File) Short Url https://arab.news/jsu85 Infant contracted infection after undergoing circumcision at illegal hospital in Karachi, says health official Experts warn of local transmission as health records show 19 cases reported between late September to October Updated 24 October 2026 Arab News Pakistan October 24, 2026 10:55 KARACHI: A 53-day-old infant died in Pakistan’s Karachi city on Saturday after contracting monkeypox (mpox) disease, health officials said, raising this year’s death toll from the infection in the southern Sindh province to 12. The infant, a resident of Karachi’s Malir district, underwent a circumcision procedure at the Ibn-e-Hassan hospital in when he was 20 days old and developed symptoms four days later, according to health officials. He was subsequently transferred to the Sindh Infectious Diseases Hospital and Research Center (SIDH&RC) at Karachi’s NIPA area, where he died on Saturday, the hospital’s medical superintendent Dr. Abdul Wahid Rajput told Arab News. Mpox virus can spread to humans from an animal host, such as rodents or non-human primates like monkeys, and from humans themselves. A person infected with mpox suffers from flu-like symptoms and an itchy, painful rash. While historically localized to parts of Africa, a global outbreak in 2022 led to a surge in cases worldwide. “The child was 53 days old and had been transferred to our hospital from a private health care facility. The cause of death was mpox,” Dr. Rajput told Arab News. The Sindh Healthcare Commission (SHCC) said it has sealed Ibn-e-Hassan hospital in Karachi’s Malir after receiving a report from a district health officer confirming the infant contracted the disease from there. According to the commission, the child contracted mpox after circumcision was performed using contaminated scissors at the facility. It said a sample tested at a laboratory returned positive for the virus. The commission said its inspection found that the hospital was operating illegally, with unqualified staff and inadequate infection prevention and control measures in place. “The action was taken over poor hygiene, inadequate infection prevention and control measures, and reported malpractices that raised serious concerns about patient safety and public health,” the SHCC said in a statement shared with Arab News. The death comes amid concerns over local transmission of mpox in Sindh. Health department surveillance records obtained by Arab News show that 19 mpox cases were reported between late September and October in the province, including infections linked to close contact, family gatherings and suspected exposures at health care facilities. Sindh has recorded 98 mpox cases and 12 deaths so far this year, according to official data. The 19 reported mpox cases documented by the provincial health department from September to October include 12 patients from Karachi and seven from other districts in Sindh, with several investigations identifying contact between infected individuals. The records include infections that linked patients in Karachi’s Malir district and elsewhere in the province, suggesting that transmission is occurring through local contact. Seventeen of the 19 patients had no recorded travel history during the preceding 21 days, although the absence of travel does not independently establish where an infection was acquired. ’EXTREMELY DANGEROUS’ Dr. Rana Jawad Asghar, a Pakistani physician and epidemiologist, said the available information indicated local transmission. However, he said the extent of the outbreak remained difficult to determine without comprehensive epidemiological investigations. “Local transmission has either begun or is already taking place,” Dr. Asghar told Arab News. He said detailed investigations of most cases were not publicly available, making it difficult to establish whether infections were spreading within households, social networks or other settings. “Every mpox case needs to be investigated, contacts traced and links between cases mapped,” he said. Asghar called for stronger disease surveillance, outbreak investigations and contact tracing, saying the infection could still be contained through timely public health interventions. He also expressed serious concern over the reported infection involving surgical instruments, warning that inadequate infection-control practices could turn health care facilities into sources of disease transmission. “If a child contracted the infection through a surgical instrument, that is extremely dangerous,” he said. Asghar pointed to previous health care-associated infections involving children in Sindh and called for strict enforcement of infection prevention measures. “Hospitals must not become a source of disease transmission,” he said. “People should not go to hospitals for treatment and return with new infections.” Asghar said stronger surveillance, thorough case investigations and contact tracing remained essential to preventing further infections. “The outbreak is still at a stage where much can be done,” he said. 台湾报告 10 月份 猴痘 病例数创两年来最高 — 台北时报 - UA.NEWS

Taiwan reports highest number of mpox cases in October in two years — Taipei Times - UA.NEWS
新闻简报台湾10月报告的猴痘病例数为两年来最高,该月前19天记录了18例。其中,16 例与 2023 年 9 月在刚果民主共和国首次发现的 Clade 1b 变种有关。美国疾病控制与预防中心 (CDC) 指出,所有 16 例 Clade 1b 病例都涉及无保护的性接触,只有 2 例收到了 猴痘 疫苗接种。今年以来,台湾已报告52例猴痘病例,其中本土感染病例41例,输入病例11例。10月份的16例1b分支病例中,22例为本地病例,8例为输入性病例,其中6例来自泰国,2例来自香港。CDC 强调,完整的 疫苗接种 提供约 90% 的保护,而一剂可提供 40% 至 80% 的保护。流行病情报中心主任郭红卫警告说,1b分支跨境传播的风险正在增加,目前已有68个国家和地区出现确诊病例。该机构还敦促拥有多个性伴侣的个人、性工作者以及在商业场所从事性活动的人使用 疫苗接种。国家资助的猴痘 疫苗接种可在台湾327家医疗机构使用。
查看新闻五要素
时间
台湾10月前19天录得18宗猴痘病例,为近两年来单月最高数字(N1)。连续七周每周都有本地感染病例报告(N2)。截至周一,台湾自年初以来已记录 52 例 猴痘 病例(N3)。
地点与对象
台湾10月有18例,年初以来有52例,其中本土感染41例,输入性病例11例(N3)。10月份的16例1b分支病例中,22例为本土病例,8例为输入性病例,其中6例来自泰国,2例来自香港(N6)。
事件
台湾10月报告猴痘病例数为两年来最高,共有18例,其中16例为Clade 1b。美国疾病控制与预防中心 (CDC) 确认了这些病例,并指出所有 16 例 1b 分支病例均涉及无保护性接触(N1、N2、N4、N7)。
规模、影响与风险
截至 10 月,记录了 18 例 猴痘 病例,其中 16 例属于分支 1b。CDC 估计,完整的 疫苗接种 可提供约 90% 的保护,而一剂可提供 40% 至 80% 的保护(N1、N13)。郭宏伟提到,1b分支跨境传播的风险正在加大,已有68个国家和地区(N9)出现确诊病例。
应对、状态与不确定性
美国疾病控制与预防中心(CDC)敦促符合疫苗接种条件的人群完成两剂疫苗疫苗接种课程,特别是那些有多名性伴侣、性工作者或曾在商业场所发生性行为的人群(N11,N12,N15)。国家资助的猴痘 疫苗接种可在327家医疗机构获得(N14)。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Taiwan reports highest number of mpox cases in October in two years — Taipei Times - UA.NEWS

News Brief
Taiwan reported the highest number of mpox cases in October in two years, with 18 cases recorded in the first 19 days of the month. Of these, 16 were linked to the Clade 1b variant, first identified in the Democratic Republic of the Congo in September 2023. The Centers for Disease Control and Prevention (CDC) noted that all 16 Clade 1b cases involved unprotected sexual contact, and only two had received an mpox vaccination. Since the start of the year, Taiwan has recorded 52 mpox cases, including 41 local infections and 11 imported cases. Of the 16 Clade 1b cases in October, 22 were local and 8 were imported, with 6 from Thailand and 2 from Hong Kong. The CDC emphasized that full vaccination provides about 90% protection, while one dose offers 40% to 80% protection. Guo Hongwei, director of the Center for Epidemic Intelligence, warned that the risk of cross-border spread of Clade 1b is increasing, as confirmed cases have been recorded in 68 countries and territories. The agency also urged vaccination for individuals with multiple sexual partners, sex workers, and those engaging in sexual activity at commercial venues. State-funded mpox vaccination is available at 327 medical institutions in Taiwan.
Original source text (English)
News Health Taiwan reports highest number of mpox cases in October in two years — Taipei Times Fedir Kryshtovskyi 22 October 2026 02:53 Taiwan recorded 18 mpox cases in the first 19 days of October, the highest monthly figure in the past two years. Local infection cases have been reported every week for seven consecutive weeks, Taipei Times reports , citing Taiwan’s Centers for Disease Control and Prevention. Since the beginning of the year, as of Monday, Taiwan has recorded 52 mpox cases: 41 infections were detected within the country, while another 11 cases were imported. According to Guo Hongwei, director of the Center for Epidemic Intelligence, 16 of the 18 cases registered in October involved the Clade 1b virus variant, which was first identified in the Democratic Republic of the Congo in September 2023. Clade 1b cases In total, 30 people have tested positive for Clade 1b this year. Of these, 22 cases were local and eight were imported: six from Thailand and two from Hong Kong. According to contact tracing data, the 16 people with Clade 1b registered in October were between 20 and 50 years old, all had unprotected sexual contact, and only two had received an mpox vaccination. More current news is available on the UA.News Telegram channel Telegram . Guo Hongwei said that the risk of cross-border spread of Clade 1b is growing, as confirmed cases of this variant have been recorded in 68 countries and territories. He also drew attention to an increase in mpox cases in some countries of the WHO Western Pacific Region. Vaccination recommendations Taiwan’s CDC said that the number of cases recorded this year is about 20% higher than during the same period last year. The agency urged people eligible for vaccination to complete the two-dose vaccine course. According to the CDC’s estimate, full vaccination provides about 90% protection, while one dose provides from 40% to 80%. State-funded mpox vaccination in Taiwan is available at 327 medical institutions. The CDC urged vaccination for people who had sexual contact with multiple partners, engaged in sex work, or had sex at commercial venues. Read us on Download our app

卫生区报告克拉克县居民首例 Clade I 猴痘 病例 - 雅虎

新闻简报内华达州南部卫生区于 2026 年 10 月 18 日在克拉克县居民中确认了第一例进化枝 I 猴痘 病例。这标志着 猴痘 病例最近有所增加,自 2026 年 8 月以来报告了 11 例确诊的进化枝 II 病例。自 2022 年 6 月第一例病例以来,卫生区已记录了 365 例 猴痘 病例。自 2024 年以来,中非和东非也出现了 暴发,其他国家(包括美国)也发现了病例。该学区强调 疫苗接种 的重要性,特别是对于 18 岁以上的人,并建议可能接触过的个人立即寻求指导。预防措施包括避免密切接触、戴口罩和隔离直至所有疮口愈合。该新闻稿强调,猴痘 通过密切的身体接触(包括性接触)传播,并且可以通过 JYNNEOS 疫苗 进行预防。卫生区鼓励有风险的人接种疫苗,并鼓励接触过的人进行 21 天的症状监测。
